        /**
         * Get country flag emoji based on destination name
         * @param {string} destination - City or country name (Hebrew or English)
         * @returns {string} Flag emoji or empty string
         */
        function getCountryFlag(destination) {
            if (!destination) return '';
            
            const lowerDest = destination.toLowerCase().trim();
            
            // Direct lookup
            if (countryFlags[destination]) {
                return countryFlags[destination];
            }
            if (countryFlags[lowerDest]) {
                return countryFlags[lowerDest];
            }
            
            // Partial match - check if any key is contained in the destination
            for (const [key, flag] of Object.entries(countryFlags)) {
                if (lowerDest.includes(key.toLowerCase()) || destination.includes(key)) {
                    return flag;
                }
            }
            
            return '';
        }

                    totalPrice += convertCurrency(f.price, f.currency, 'ILS', f.customExchangeRate);
                } else {
                    totalPrice += (f.basePrice || 0) + (f.markup || 0);
                }
            });
            state.hotels.forEach(h => {
                if (h.price && h.currency) {
                    totalPrice += convertCurrency(h.price, h.currency, 'ILS', h.customExchangeRate);
                } else {
                    totalPrice += (h.basePrice || 0) + (h.markup || 0);
                }
            });
            // Add services to total price
            state.services.forEach(s => {
                if (s.price && s.currency) {
                    totalPrice += convertCurrency(s.price, s.currency, 'ILS', s.customExchangeRate);
                } else {
                    totalPrice += s.price || 0;
                }
            });
            const priceInUSD = convertCurrency(totalPrice, 'ILS', 'USD');
            
            // Format prices with thousand separators
            const priceInILSFormatted = totalPrice.toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: 0, maximumFractionDigits: 0 });
            const priceInUSDFormatted = priceInUSD.toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 });
            
            // Get dynamic flag based on destination
            const destFlag = getCountryFlag(destination);
            
            // Build ALL layovers text for outbound
            let outboundLayoversText = '';
            if (outboundLayovers.length > 0) {
                outboundLayoversText = outboundLayovers.map(l => `${l.city} | ${l.duration}`).join('\n🕒 עצירת ביניים: ');
            }
            
            // Build ALL layovers text for return
            let returnLayoversText = '';
            if (returnLayovers.length > 0) {
                returnLayoversText = returnLayovers.map(l => `${l.city} | ${l.duration}`).join('\n🕒 עצירת ביניים: ');
            }
            
            // Fill template - need to handle outbound and return layovers separately
            let proposal = state.flightProposalTemplate;
            
            // Replace flags - replace hardcoded flags with dynamic destination flag
            // Handle common patterns like 🇮🇱🇨🇷 or 🇮🇱🇹🇭 etc.
            proposal = proposal.replace(/🇮🇱[\u{1F1E0}-\u{1F1FF}]{2}/gu, `🇮🇱${destFlag}`);
            // Also replace standalone destination flags if present
            proposal = proposal.replace(/🇨🇷|🇹🇭|🇬🇷|🇮🇹|🇪🇸|🇫🇷|🇹🇷/g, destFlag || '');
            
            // Replace common fields
            proposal = proposal
                .replace(/\[יעד\]/g, destination)
                .replace(/\[תאריך יציאה\]/g, departureDate)
                .replace(/\[תאריך חזרה\]/g, returnDate)
                .replace(/\[מספר לילות\]/g, nights > 0 ? nights.toString() : '')
                .replace(/\[חברת תעופה\]/g, airline)
                .replace(/\[משקל ק"ג\]/g, baggageWeight)
                .replace(/\[פרטי מלונות\]/g, hotelsText + servicesText || '')
                .replace(/\[מחיר בשקלים\]/g, priceInILSFormatted)
                .replace(/\[מחיר בדולרים\]/g, priceInUSDFormatted)
                .replace(/\[מחיר\]/g, `${priceInILSFormatted}₪ / ${priceInUSDFormatted}$`) // Fallback for old template format
                .replace(/\[הערות נוספות בנוגע לזמינות הכרטיסים או תנאי הכרטוס\]/g, '');

            proposal = proposal
                .replace(/\[מסלול טיסה הלוך עם שעות\]/g, outboundRoute || '[לא זמין]')
                .replace(/(✈️ \*הלוך:\*[\s\S]*?🕒 עצירת ביניים: )\[יעד עצירה\]( \| )\[משך עצירה\]/g,

                    `$1${outboundTotalTime || ''}`);
            
            // Replace return section with ALL layovers
            proposal = proposal
                .replace(/\[מסלול טיסה חזור עם שעות\]/g, returnRoute || '[לא זמין]')
                .replace(/(✈️ \*חזור:\*[\s\S]*?🕒 עצירת ביניים: )\[יעד עצירה\]( \| )\[משך עצירה\]/g,

  old_string:         function generateFlightProposalText() {
            if (state.flights.length === 0) {
                showToast('❌ אין טיסות להצגה');
                return;
            }
            
            // Collect all flight segments
            let allFlightSegments = [];
            state.flights.forEach(flight => {
                if (flight.extractedData?.flights) {
                    flight.extractedData.flights.forEach(f => {
                        allFlightSegments.push(f);
                    });
                }
            });
            
            if (allFlightSegments.length === 0) {
                showToast('❌ אין פרטי טיסות להצגה');
                return;
            }
            
            // Fix date rollover and validate chain
            allFlightSegments = validateAndFixFlightChain(allFlightSegments);
            
            // Sort by timestamp
            allFlightSegments.sort((a, b) => {
                const timestampA = getSegmentISOTimestamp(a);
                const timestampB = getSegmentISOTimestamp(b);
                return timestampA - timestampB;
            });
            
            // Group into journeys
            const journeys = groupFlightsByJourney(allFlightSegments);
            
            // Find destination (last airport in outbound journey)
            let destination = '';
            const outboundJourney = journeys.find(j => j.type === 'הלוך');
            if (outboundJourney && outboundJourney.segments.length > 0) {
                const lastSegment = outboundJourney.segments[outboundJourney.segments.length - 1];
                destination = lastSegment.toCity || lastSegment.to || 'יעד';
            }
            
            // Find dates
            let departureDate = '';
            let returnDate = '';
            if (outboundJourney && outboundJourney.segments.length > 0) {
                departureDate = formatShortDate(outboundJourney.segments[0].date) || '';
            }
            const returnJourney = journeys.find(j => j.type === 'חזור');
            if (returnJourney && returnJourney.segments.length > 0) {
                const lastReturnSegment = returnJourney.segments[returnJourney.segments.length - 1];
                returnDate = formatShortDate(lastReturnSegment.arrivalDate || lastReturnSegment.date) || '';
            }
            
            // Calculate nights
            let nights = 0;
            if (departureDate && returnDate) {
                try {
                    const depMatch = departureDate.match(/(\d{2})\.(\d{2})/);
                    const retMatch = returnDate.match(/(\d{2})\.(\d{2})/);
                    if (depMatch && retMatch) {
                        const depDay = parseInt(depMatch[1]);
                        const depMonth = parseInt(depMatch[2]);
                        const retDay = parseInt(retMatch[1]);
                        const retMonth = parseInt(retMatch[2]);
                        const year = new Date().getFullYear();
                        const depDate = new Date(year, depMonth - 1, depDay);
                        const retDate = new Date(year, retMonth - 1, retDay);
                        const diffTime = retDate - depDate;
                        nights = Math.ceil(diffTime / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
                    }
                } catch (e) {
                    console.warn('Could not calculate nights:', e);
                }
            }
            
            // Find airline (most common airline)
            const airlines = {};
            allFlightSegments.forEach(seg => {
                if (seg.airline) {
                    airlines[seg.airline] = (airlines[seg.airline] || 0) + 1;
                }
            });
            const airline = Object.keys(airlines).sort((a, b) => airlines[b] - airlines[a])[0] || 'חברת תעופה';
            
            // Build flight routes with ALL connections
            let outboundRoute = '';
            let outboundLayovers = []; // Changed to array for multiple connections
            let outboundTotalTime = '';
            if (outboundJourney) {
                const segments = outboundJourney.segments;
                segments.forEach((seg, idx) => {
                    const from = seg.fromCity || seg.from || '';
                    const to = seg.toCity || seg.to || '';
                    const depTime = seg.departureTime || '';
                    const arrTime = seg.arrivalTime || '';
                    const date = formatShortDate(seg.date) || '';
                    const arrDate = formatShortDate(seg.arrivalDate || seg.date) || '';
                    
                    if (idx === 0) {
                        outboundRoute += `${from} ${depTime} (${date}) → `;
                    }
                    if (idx < segments.length - 1) {
                        outboundRoute += `${to} ${arrTime} (${arrDate})\n`;
                        const nextSeg = segments[idx + 1];
                        const layover = calculateConnection(seg.arrivalTime, nextSeg.departureTime);
                        if (layover) {
                            // Collect ALL layovers, not just the last one
                            outboundLayovers.push({
                                city: to,
                                duration: layover.formatted
                            });
                        }
                    } else {
                        outboundRoute += `${to} ${arrTime} (${arrDate})`;
                    }
                });
                // Calculate total time (rough estimate)
                if (segments.length > 0) {
                    const first = segments[0];
                    const last = segments[segments.length - 1];
                    const depTime = parseTimeForSort(first.departureTime);
                    const arrTime = parseTimeForSort(last.arrivalTime);
                    let totalMins = arrTime - depTime;
                    if (totalMins < 0) totalMins += 24 * 60;
                    const hours = Math.floor(totalMins / 60);
                    const mins = totalMins % 60;
                    outboundTotalTime = `${hours} שעות ${mins > 0 ? `${mins} דקות` : ''}`;
                }
            }
            
            let returnRoute = '';
            let returnLayovers = []; // Changed to array for multiple connections
            let returnTotalTime = '';
            if (returnJourney) {
                const segments = returnJourney.segments;
                segments.forEach((seg, idx) => {
                    const from = seg.fromCity || seg.from || '';
                    const to = seg.toCity || seg.to || '';
                    const depTime = seg.departureTime || '';
                    const arrTime = seg.arrivalTime || '';
                    const date = formatShortDate(seg.date) || '';
                    const arrDate = formatShortDate(seg.arrivalDate || seg.date) || '';
                    
                    if (idx === 0) {
                        returnRoute += `${from} ${depTime} (${date}) → `;
                    }
                    if (idx < segments.length - 1) {
                        returnRoute += `${to} ${arrTime} (${arrDate})\n`;
                        const nextSeg = segments[idx + 1];
                        const layover = calculateConnection(seg.arrivalTime, nextSeg.departureTime);
                        if (layover) {
                            // Collect ALL layovers, not just the last one
                            returnLayovers.push({
                                city: to,
                                duration: layover.formatted
                            });
                        }
                    } else {
                        returnRoute += `${to} ${arrTime} (${arrDate})`;
                    }
                });
                // Calculate total time
                if (segments.length > 0) {
                    const first = segments[0];
                    const last = segments[segments.length - 1];
                    const depTime = parseTimeForSort(first.departureTime);
                    const arrTime = parseTimeForSort(last.arrivalTime);
                    let totalMins = arrTime - depTime;
                    if (totalMins < 0) totalMins += 24 * 60;
                    const hours = Math.floor(totalMins / 60);
                    const mins = totalMins % 60;
                    returnTotalTime = `${hours} שעות ${mins > 0 ? `${mins} דקות` : ''}`;
                }
            }
            
            // Get baggage info
            const baggageInfo = document.getElementById('baggageInfo')?.value || '23 ק"ג';
            const baggageMatch = baggageInfo.match(/(\d+)/);
            const baggageWeight = baggageMatch ? baggageMatch[1] : '23';
            
            // Build hotels section with booking links
            let hotelsText = '';
            if (state.hotels.length > 0) {
                // Sort hotels by check-in date
                const sortedHotels = [...state.hotels].filter(h => h.extractedData).sort((a, b) => {
                    const dateA = parseDateForSort(a.extractedData?.checkIn);
                    const dateB = parseDateForSort(b.extractedData?.checkIn);
                    return dateA - dateB;
                });
                
                if (sortedHotels.length > 0) {
                    hotelsText = '\n*🏨 מלונות:*\n';
                    sortedHotels.forEach((hotel, idx) => {
                        const h = hotel.extractedData || {};
                        const hotelName = h.hotelName || 'מלון';
                        // Translate location to Hebrew
                        const location = h.location ? translateLocation(h.location) : '';
                        const checkIn = h.checkIn || '';
                        const checkOut = h.checkOut || '';
                        const nights = h.nights || '';
                        // Translate room type to Hebrew
                        const roomType = h.roomType ? translateRoomType(h.roomType) : '';
                        // Translate board basis to Hebrew
                        const boardBasis = h.boardBasis ? translateBoardBasis(h.boardBasis) : '';
                        const rating = h.rating || h.stars || '';
                        // Get booking URL if available
                        const bookingUrl = h.bookingUrl || '';
                        
                        hotelsText += `\n🏨 *${hotelName}*`;
                        if (location) hotelsText += `\n📍 ${location}`;
                        if (checkIn && checkOut) {
                            hotelsText += `\n📅 ${checkIn} - ${checkOut}`;
                            if (nights) hotelsText += ` (${nights} לילות)`;
                        }
                        if (roomType) hotelsText += `\n🛏️ ${roomType}`;
                        if (boardBasis) hotelsText += `\n🍽️ ${boardBasis}`;
                        if (rating) {
                            const stars = '★'.repeat(Math.floor(rating));
                            hotelsText += `\n⭐ ${stars}`;
                        }
                        // Add Booking.com link if available
                        if (bookingUrl) {
                            hotelsText += `\n🔗 קישור להזמנה: ${bookingUrl}`;
                        }
                        if (idx < sortedHotels.length - 1) hotelsText += '\n';
                    });
                }
            }
            
            // Build services/extras section
            let servicesText = '';
            if (state.services.length > 0) {
                servicesText = '\n\n*🎫 תוספות ושירותים:*\n';
                state.services.forEach((service, idx) => {
                    const name = service.name || 'שירות';
                    const description = service.description || '';
                    const price = service.price || 0;
                    const currency = service.currency || 'ILS';
                    const priceInILS = convertCurrency(price, currency, 'ILS', service.customExchangeRate);
                    
                    servicesText += `\n🎫 *${name}*`;
                    if (description) {
                        servicesText += `\n   ${description}`;
                    }
                    servicesText += `\n   💰 ${priceInILS.toLocaleString('en-US', {minimumFractionDigits: 0, maximumFractionDigits: 0})}₪`;
                    if (idx < state.services.length - 1) servicesText += '\n';
                });
            }
            
            // Calculate total price (flights + hotels + services)
            let totalPrice = 0;
            state.flights.forEach(f => {
                if (f.price && f.currency) {
                    totalPrice += convertCurrency(f.price, f.currency, 'ILS', f.customExchangeRate);
                } else {
                    totalPrice += (f.basePrice || 0) + (f.markup || 0);
                }
            });
            state.hotels.forEach(h => {
                if (h.price && h.currency) {
                    totalPrice += convertCurrency(h.price, h.currency, 'ILS', h.customExchangeRate);
                } else {
                    totalPrice += (h.basePrice || 0) + (h.markup || 0);
                }
            });
            // Add services to total price
            state.services.forEach(s => {
                if (s.price && s.currency) {
                    totalPrice += convertCurrency(s.price, s.currency, 'ILS', s.customExchangeRate);
                } else {
                    totalPrice += s.price || 0;
                }
            });
            const priceInUSD = convertCurrency(totalPrice, 'ILS', 'USD');
            
            // Format prices with thousand separators
            const priceInILSFormatted = totalPrice.toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: 0, maximumFractionDigits: 0 });
            const priceInUSDFormatted = priceInUSD.toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 });
            
            // Get dynamic flag based on destination
            const destFlag = getCountryFlag(destination);
            
            // Build ALL layovers text for outbound

            if (outboundLayovers.length > 0) {
                outboundLayoversText = outboundLayovers.map(l => `${l.city} | ${l.duration}`).join('\n🕒 עצירת ביניים: ');
            }
            
            // Build ALL layovers text for return
            let returnLayoversText = '';
            if (returnLayovers.length > 0) {
                returnLayoversText = returnLayovers.map(l => `${l.city} | ${l.duration}`).join('\n🕒 עצירת ביניים: ');
            }
            
            // Fill template - need to handle outbound and return layovers separately
            let proposal = state.flightProposalTemplate;
            
            // Replace flags - replace hardcoded flags with dynamic destination flag
            // Handle common patterns like 🇮🇱🇨🇷 or 🇮🇱🇹🇭 etc.
            proposal = proposal.replace(/🇮🇱[\u{1F1E0}-\u{1F1FF}]{2}/gu, `🇮🇱${destFlag}`);
            // Also replace standalone destination flags if present
            proposal = proposal.replace(/🇨🇷|🇹🇭|🇬🇷|🇮🇹|🇪🇸|🇫🇷|🇹🇷/g, destFlag || '');
            
            // Replace common fields
            proposal = proposal
                .replace(/\[יעד\]/g, destination)
                .replace(/\[תאריך יציאה\]/g, departureDate)
                .replace(/\[תאריך חזרה\]/g, returnDate)
                .replace(/\[מספר לילות\]/g, nights > 0 ? nights.toString() : '')
                .replace(/\[חברת תעופה\]/g, airline)
                .replace(/\[משקל ק"ג\]/g, baggageWeight)
                .replace(/\[פרטי מלונות\]/g, hotelsText + servicesText || '')
                .replace(/\[מחיר בשקלים\]/g, priceInILSFormatted)
                .replace(/\[מחיר בדולרים\]/g, priceInUSDFormatted)
                .replace(/\[מחיר\]/g, `${priceInILSFormatted}₪ / ${priceInUSDFormatted}$`) // Fallback for old template format
                .replace(/\[הערות נוספות בנוגע לזמינות הכרטיסים או תנאי הכרטוס\]/g, '');
            
            // Replace outbound section with ALL layovers
            proposal = proposal
                .replace(/\[מסלול טיסה הלוך עם שעות\]/g, outboundRoute || '[לא זמין]')
                .replace(/(✈️ \*הלוך:\*[\s\S]*?🕒 עצירת ביניים: )\[יעד עצירה\]( \| )\[משך עצירה\]/g, 
                    outboundLayoversText ? `$1${outboundLayoversText}` : '$1ללא עצירות')
                .replace(/(✈️ \*הלוך:\*[\s\S]*?⏳ משך זמן כולל: )\[סה"כ זמן נסיעה\]/g,
                    `$1${outboundTotalTime || ''}`);
            
            // Replace return section with ALL layovers
            proposal = proposal
                .replace(/\[מסלול טיסה חזור עם שעות\]/g, returnRoute || '[לא זמין]')
                .replace(/(✈️ \*חזור:\*[\s\S]*?🕒 עצירת ביניים: )\[יעד עצירה\]( \| )\[משך עצירה\]/g,
                    returnLayoversText ? `$1${returnLayoversText}` : '$1ללא עצירות')
                .replace(/(✈️ \*חזור:\*[\s\S]*?⏳ משך זמן כולל: )\[סה"כ זמן נסיעה\]/g,
                    `$1${returnTotalTime || ''}`);
            
            // Clean up any remaining placeholders
            proposal = proposal.replace(/\[[^\]]+\]/g, '');
            
            // Copy to clipboard
            navigator.clipboard.writeText(proposal).then(() => {
                showToast('📋 הצעת טיסות הועתקה ללוח');
            }).catch(() => {
                const ta = document.createElement('textarea');
                ta.value = proposal;
                document.body.appendChild(ta);
                ta.select();
                document.execCommand('copy');
                document.body.removeChild(ta);
                showToast('📋 הצעת טיסות הועתקה ללוח');
            });
            
            // Also show in console for debugging
            console.log('Flight Proposal Text:', proposal);
        }
  new_string:         function generateFlightProposalText() {
            if (state.flights.length === 0) {
                showToast('❌ אין טיסות להצגה');
                return;
            }
            
            // Collect all flight segments
            let allFlightSegments = [];
            state.flights.forEach(flight => {
                if (flight.extractedData?.flights) {
                    flight.extractedData.flights.forEach(f => {
                        allFlightSegments.push(f);
                    });
                }
            });
            
            if (allFlightSegments.length === 0) {
                showToast('❌ אין פרטי טיסות להצגה');
                return;
            }
            
            // Fix date rollover and validate chain
            allFlightSegments = validateAndFixFlightChain(allFlightSegments);
            
            // Sort by timestamp
            allFlightSegments.sort((a, b) => {
                const timestampA = getSegmentISOTimestamp(a);
                const timestampB = getSegmentISOTimestamp(b);
                return timestampA - timestampB;
            });
            
            // Group into journeys
            const journeys = groupFlightsByJourney(allFlightSegments);
            
            // Find destination (last airport in outbound journey)
            let destination = '';
            const outboundJourney = journeys.find(j => j.type === 'הלוך');
            if (outboundJourney && outboundJourney.segments.length > 0) {
                const lastSegment = outboundJourney.segments[outboundJourney.segments.length - 1];
                destination = lastSegment.toCity || lastSegment.to || 'יעד';
            }
            
            // Get dynamic flag based on destination
            const destFlag = getCountryFlag(destination);
            
            // Find dates
            let departureDate = '';
            let returnDate = '';
            let departureDateFull = '';
            let returnDateFull = '';
            if (outboundJourney && outboundJourney.segments.length > 0) {
                departureDate = formatShortDate(outboundJourney.segments[0].date) || '';
                departureDateFull = outboundJourney.segments[0].date || '';
            }
            const returnJourney = journeys.find(j => j.type === 'חזור');
            if (returnJourney && returnJourney.segments.length > 0) {
                const lastReturnSegment = returnJourney.segments[returnJourney.segments.length - 1];
                returnDate = formatShortDate(lastReturnSegment.arrivalDate || lastReturnSegment.date) || '';
                returnDateFull = lastReturnSegment.arrivalDate || lastReturnSegment.date || '';
            }
            
            // Calculate nights
            let nights = 0;
            if (departureDate && returnDate) {
                try {
                    const depMatch = departureDate.match(/(\d{2})\.(\d{2})/);
                    const retMatch = returnDate.match(/(\d{2})\.(\d{2})/);
                    if (depMatch && retMatch) {
                        const depDay = parseInt(depMatch[1]);
                        const depMonth = parseInt(depMatch[2]);
                        const retDay = parseInt(retMatch[1]);
                        const retMonth = parseInt(retMatch[2]);
                        const year = new Date().getFullYear();
                        const depDate = new Date(year, depMonth - 1, depDay);
                        const retDate = new Date(year, retMonth - 1, retDay);
                        const diffTime = retDate - depDate;
                        nights = Math.ceil(diffTime / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
                    }
                } catch (e) {
                    console.warn('Could not calculate nights:', e);
                }
            }
            
            // Find airline (most common airline)
            const airlines = {};
            allFlightSegments.forEach(seg => {
                if (seg.airline) {
                    airlines[seg.airline] = (airlines[seg.airline] || 0) + 1;
                }
            });
            const airline = Object.keys(airlines).sort((a, b) => airlines[b] - airlines[a])[0] || 'חברת תעופה';
            
            // Get baggage info
            const baggageInfo = document.getElementById('baggageInfo')?.value || 'תיק יד + מזוודה 23 ק״ג';
            
            // Build outbound flight details with ALL connections
            let outboundText = '';
            let outboundTotalTime = '';
            if (outboundJourney) {
                const segments = outboundJourney.segments;
                segments.forEach((seg, idx) => {
                    const from = seg.fromCity || seg.from || '';
                    const to = seg.toCity || seg.to || '';
                    const depTime = seg.departureTime || '';
                    const arrTime = seg.arrivalTime || '';
                    const flightNum = seg.flightNumber || '';
                    const segAirline = seg.airline || airline;
                    
                    // Add flight segment
                    outboundText += `${from} ${depTime} → ${to} ${arrTime}`;
                    if (flightNum) outboundText += ` (${segAirline} ${flightNum})`;
                    outboundText += '\n';
                    
                    // Add layover info if not the last segment
                    if (idx < segments.length - 1) {
                        const nextSeg = segments[idx + 1];
                        const layover = calculateConnection(seg.arrivalTime, nextSeg.departureTime);
                        if (layover) {
                            outboundText += `🕒 עצירת ביניים: ${to} | ${layover.formatted}\n`;
                        }
                    }
                });
                
                // Calculate total time
                if (segments.length > 0) {
                    const first = segments[0];
                    const last = segments[segments.length - 1];
                    const depTime = parseTimeForSort(first.departureTime);
                    const arrTime = parseTimeForSort(last.arrivalTime);
                    let totalMins = arrTime - depTime;
                    if (totalMins < 0) totalMins += 24 * 60;
                    const hours = Math.floor(totalMins / 60);
                    const mins = totalMins % 60;
                    outboundTotalTime = `${hours} שעות${mins > 0 ? ` ${mins} דקות` : ''}`;
                }
            }
            
            // Build return flight details with ALL connections
            let returnText = '';
            let returnTotalTime = '';
            if (returnJourney) {
                const segments = returnJourney.segments;
                segments.forEach((seg, idx) => {
                    const from = seg.fromCity || seg.from || '';
                    const to = seg.toCity || seg.to || '';
                    const depTime = seg.departureTime || '';
                    const arrTime = seg.arrivalTime || '';
                    const flightNum = seg.flightNumber || '';
                    const segAirline = seg.airline || airline;
                    
                    // Add flight segment
                    returnText += `${from} ${depTime} → ${to} ${arrTime}`;
                    if (flightNum) returnText += ` (${segAirline} ${flightNum})`;
                    returnText += '\n';
                    
                    // Add layover info if not the last segment
                    if (idx < segments.length - 1) {
                        const nextSeg = segments[idx + 1];
                        const layover = calculateConnection(seg.arrivalTime, nextSeg.departureTime);
                        if (layover) {
                            returnText += `🕒 עצירת ביניים: ${to} | ${layover.formatted}\n`;
                        }
                    }
                });
                
                // Calculate total time
                if (segments.length > 0) {
                    const first = segments[0];
                    const last = segments[segments.length - 1];
                    const depTime = parseTimeForSort(first.departureTime);
                    const arrTime = parseTimeForSort(last.arrivalTime);
                    let totalMins = arrTime - depTime;
                    if (totalMins < 0) totalMins += 24 * 60;
                    const hours = Math.floor(totalMins / 60);
                    const mins = totalMins % 60;
                    returnTotalTime = `${hours} שעות${mins > 0 ? ` ${mins} דקות` : ''}`;
                }
            }
            
            // Build hotels section with booking links
            let hotelsText = '';
            if (state.hotels.length > 0) {
                const sortedHotels = [...state.hotels].filter(h => h.extractedData).sort((a, b) => {
                    const dateA = parseDateForSort(a.extractedData?.checkIn);
                    const dateB = parseDateForSort(b.extractedData?.checkIn);
                    return dateA - dateB;
                });
                
                if (sortedHotels.length > 0) {
                    hotelsText = '\n*🏨 מלונות:*\n';
                    sortedHotels.forEach((hotel, idx) => {
                        const h = hotel.extractedData || {};
                        const hotelName = h.hotelName || 'מלון';
                        const location = h.location ? translateLocation(h.location) : '';
                        const checkIn = h.checkIn || '';
                        const checkOut = h.checkOut || '';
                        const hotelNights = h.nights || '';
                        const roomType = h.roomType ? translateRoomType(h.roomType) : '';
                        const boardBasis = h.boardBasis ? translateBoardBasis(h.boardBasis) : '';
                        const rating = h.rating || h.stars || '';
                        const bookingUrl = h.bookingUrl || '';
                        
                        hotelsText += `\n🏨 *${hotelName}*`;
                        if (location) hotelsText += `\n📍 ${location}`;
                        if (checkIn && checkOut) {
                            hotelsText += `\n📅 ${checkIn} - ${checkOut}`;
                            if (hotelNights) hotelsText += ` (${hotelNights} לילות)`;
                        }
                        if (roomType) hotelsText += `\n🛏️ ${roomType}`;
                        if (boardBasis) hotelsText += `\n🍽️ ${boardBasis}`;
                        if (rating) {
                            const stars = '★'.repeat(Math.floor(rating));
                            hotelsText += `\n⭐ ${stars}`;
                        }
                        if (bookingUrl) {
                            hotelsText += `\n🔗 קישור להזמנה: ${bookingUrl}`;
                        }
                        if (idx < sortedHotels.length - 1) hotelsText += '\n';
                    });
                }
            }
            
            // Build services/extras section
            let servicesText = '';
            if (state.services.length > 0) {
                servicesText = '\n\n*🎫 תוספות ושירותים:*';
                state.services.forEach((service, idx) => {
                    const name = service.name || 'שירות';
                    const description = service.description || '';
                    const price = service.price || 0;
                    const currency = service.currency || 'ILS';
                    const priceInILS = convertCurrency(price, currency, 'ILS', service.customExchangeRate);
                    
                    servicesText += `\n\n🎫 *${name}*`;
                    if (description) {
                        servicesText += `\n${description}`;
                    }
                    servicesText += `\n💰 ${priceInILS.toLocaleString('en-US', {minimumFractionDigits: 0, maximumFractionDigits: 0})}₪`;
                });
            }
            
            // Calculate total price (flights + hotels + services)
            let totalPrice = 0;
            state.flights.forEach(f => {
                if (f.price && f.currency) {
                    totalPrice += convertCurrency(f.price, f.currency, 'ILS', f.customExchangeRate);
                } else {
                    totalPrice += (f.basePrice || 0) + (f.markup || 0);
                }
            });
            state.hotels.forEach(h => {
                if (h.price && h.currency) {
                    totalPrice += convertCurrency(h.price, h.currency, 'ILS', h.customExchangeRate);
                } else {
                    totalPrice += (h.basePrice || 0) + (h.markup || 0);
                }
            });
            state.services.forEach(s => {
                if (s.price && s.currency) {
                    totalPrice += convertCurrency(s.price, s.currency, 'ILS', s.customExchangeRate);
                } else {
                    totalPrice += s.price || 0;
                }
            });
            const priceInUSD = convertCurrency(totalPrice, 'ILS', 'USD');
            
            const priceInILSFormatted = totalPrice.toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: 0, maximumFractionDigits: 0 });
            const priceInUSDFormatted = priceInUSD.toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 });
            
            // Build proposal text directly (not using template)
            let proposal = `*✈️ הצעה לטיסות ${destination} 🇮🇱${destFlag}*

*תאריכים:*
📅 הלוך: ${departureDate}
📅 חזור: ${returnDate}
סה"כ: ${nights > 0 ? nights + ' לילות' : ''}

*טיסות:*
חברת תעופה: ✈️ ${airline}

✈️ *הלוך:*
${outboundText}⏳ משך זמן כולל: ${outboundTotalTime}

✈️ *חזור:*
${returnText}⏳ משך זמן כולל: ${returnTotalTime}

*כבודה: 🧳*
${baggageInfo}
${hotelsText}${servicesText}

*תנאי ביטול:*
הכרטיס אינו גמיש ויהיה קנס על כל ביטול/שינוי
- במידה וחברת התעופה מבטלת את הטיסה כמובן שהכסף שב אליכם חזרה

*מה אני נותן בתור שירות:*
✅ טיפול בהזמנה וביטולים במידת הצורך
✅ עזרה ותמיכה מתמדת בכל שאלה
✅ חיסכון בעמלות מטבע – אפשרות תשלום בשקלים

💰 *עלות כוללת:*
${priceInILSFormatted}₪ / ${priceInUSDFormatted}$ עבור נוסע בכרטיס

המחיר מוגבל לזמן ואוכל לשריין אותו עבורך. שלח לי את פרטי הדרכונים כדי שאוודא את זמינות העסקה.`;
            
            // Copy to clipboard
            navigator.clipboard.writeText(proposal).then(() => {
                showToast('📋 הצעת טיסות הועתקה ללוח');
            }).catch(() => {
                const ta = document.createElement('textarea');

                ta.select();
                document.execCommand('copy');
                document.body.removeChild(ta);
                showToast('📋 הצעת טיסות הועתקה ללוח');
            });
            
            console.log('Flight Proposal Text:', proposal);

            function cleanCityName(name) {
                if (!name) return '';
                // Remove patterns like (TLV), (AUH), etc.
                return name.replace(/\s*\([A-Z]{2,4}\)\s*/g, '').trim();
            }

                    const toCode = (seg.to || '').toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z]/g, '').substring(0, 3);

The problem likely stems from `f.customExchangeRate` not being populated correctly when the conversion is called, or the fallback logic being applied when it shouldn't be.

Looking at the screenshot more carefully, the total shown is 11,599 ₪, which should represent the sum of all converted items plus markups. Testing different multipliers against the 14,249 USD sum: multiplying by 0.75 gives 10,687, by 0.78 gives 11,114, by 0.81 gives 11,542, and by 0.82 gives 11,684. The 0.81 and 0.82 results are suspiciously close to the actual total, which suggests the conversion formula might be inverted—dividing by the rate instead of multiplying. Breaking down individual conversions this way (2400 / 3.19 = 752.35, 565 / 3.22 = 175.47, 454 / 3.22 = 141.00) would reveal whether the implementation is using division rather than multiplication.

Looking at the code more carefully, the custom rate logic only applies when all conditions are met: customRate exists, the source currency isn't ILS, and the target is ILS. This narrow condition might be preventing the custom rate from being used in certain scenarios.

When those conditions fail, the code falls back to a generic conversion that divides by exchange rates. For USD to ILS specifically, this means taking the amount, dividing by 1 (USD rate), then dividing by 0.263 (ILS rate)—which effectively multiplies by 3.8. So 2400 USD would convert to 9,120 ILS using this fallback logic, which doesn't match what the user expects.

The discrepancy suggests the custom rates might not be persisting correctly. ### 1. הוספת Event Listener להדבקה
בקובץ [index.html](index.html), להוסיף listener ל-`paste` event על ה-document:

```javascript
document.addEventListener('paste', handlePaste);
```

### 2. פונקציית handlePaste
פונקציה שתופסת את ה-paste event ובודקת אם יש תמונה ב-clipboard:

```javascript
function handlePaste(e) {
    const items = e.clipboardData?.items;
    if (!items) return;
    
    for (const item of items) {
        if (item.type.startsWith('image/')) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const file = item.getAsFile();
            showPasteTypeSelector(file);
            return;
        }
    }
}
```

        // Drop Zone Setup
        // ==========================================
        function setupDropZone(dropZoneId, inputId, type) {
            const dropZone = document.getElementById(dropZoneId);
            const input = document.getElementById(inputId);
            
            dropZone.addEventListener('click', () => input.click());
            
            dropZone.addEventListener('dragover', (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                dropZone.classList.add('dragover');
            });
            
            dropZone.addEventListener('dragleave', () => {
                dropZone.classList.remove('dragover');
            });
            
            dropZone.addEventListener('drop', (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                dropZone.classList.remove('dragover');
                handleFiles(e.dataTransfer.files, type);
            });
            
            input.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
                handleFiles(e.target.files, type);
                e.target.value = ''; // Reset for same file selection
            });
        }
  new_string:         // ==========================================
        // Drop Zone Setup
        // ==========================================
        function setupDropZone(dropZoneId, inputId, type) {
            const dropZone = document.getElementById(dropZoneId);
            const input = document.getElementById(inputId);
            
            dropZone.addEventListener('click', () => input.click());
            
            dropZone.addEventListener('dragover', (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                dropZone.classList.add('dragover');
            });
            
            dropZone.addEventListener('dragleave', () => {
                dropZone.classList.remove('dragover');
            });
            
            dropZone.addEventListener('drop', (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                dropZone.classList.remove('dragover');
                handleFiles(e.dataTransfer.files, type);
            });
            
            input.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
                handleFiles(e.target.files, type);
                e.target.value = ''; // Reset for same file selection
            });
        }
        
        // ==========================================
        // Ctrl+V Paste Handler
        // ==========================================
        function handlePaste(e) {
            const items = e.clipboardData?.items;
            if (!items) return;
            
            for (const item of items) {
                if (item.type.startsWith('image/')) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const file = item.getAsFile();
                    if (file) {
                        showPasteTypeSelector(file);
                    }
                    return;
                }
            }
        }
        
        function showPasteTypeSelector(file) {
            // Remove existing modal if any
            const existingModal = document.getElementById('pasteTypeModal');
            if (existingModal) existingModal.remove();
            
            // Create overlay
            const overlay = document.createElement('div');
            overlay.id = 'pasteTypeModal';
            overlay.className = 'paste-modal-overlay';
            overlay.innerHTML = `
                <div class="paste-modal">
                    <div class="paste-modal-title">בחר סוג תמונה</div>
                    <div class="paste-modal-preview">
                        <img src="${URL.createObjectURL(file)}" alt="Preview">
                    </div>
                    <div class="paste-modal-buttons">
                        <button class="paste-btn paste-btn-flight" data-type="flight">
                            <span class="paste-btn-icon">✈️</span>
                            <span class="paste-btn-text">טיסה</span>
                        </button>
                        <button class="paste-btn paste-btn-hotel" data-type="hotel">
                            <span class="paste-btn-icon">🏨</span>
                            <span class="paste-btn-text">מלון</span>
                        </button>
                        <button class="paste-btn paste-btn-service" data-type="service">
                            <span class="paste-btn-icon">🎫</span>
                            <span class="paste-btn-text">שירות</span>
                        </button>
                    </div>
                    <button class="paste-modal-close">✕</button>
                </div>
            `;
            
            document.body.appendChild(overlay);
            
            // Handle button clicks
            overlay.querySelectorAll('.paste-btn').forEach(btn => {
                btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                    const type = btn.dataset.type;
                    overlay.remove();
                    addImageCard(file, type);
                });
            });
            
            // Handle close button
            overlay.querySelector('.paste-modal-close').addEventListener('click', () => {
                overlay.remove();
            });
            
            // Handle click outside modal
            overlay.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                if (e.target === overlay) {
                    overlay.remove();
                }
            });
            
            // Handle ESC key
            const escHandler = (e) => {
                if (e.key === 'Escape') {
                    overlay.remove();
                    document.removeEventListener('keydown', escHandler);
                }
            };
            document.addEventListener('keydown', escHandler);
        }

  old_string:         // Currency conversion function using custom or default rates
        function convertCurrency(amount, fromCurrency, toCurrency, customRate = null) {
            if (!amount || !fromCurrency || !toCurrency) return 0;
            if (fromCurrency === toCurrency) return parseFloat(amount);
            
            // If custom rate provided, use it directly
            if (customRate && fromCurrency !== 'ILS' && toCurrency === 'ILS') {
                return parseFloat(amount) * parseFloat(customRate);
            }
            
            // Convert to USD first, then to target currency
            const fromRate = getExchangeRate(fromCurrency);
            const toRate = getExchangeRate(toCurrency);
            const amountInUSD = parseFloat(amount) / fromRate;
            const amountInTarget = amountInUSD / toRate;
            
            return amountInTarget;
        }
  new_string:         // Currency conversion function using custom or default rates
        function convertCurrency(amount, fromCurrency, toCurrency, customRate = null) {
            if (!amount || !fromCurrency || !toCurrency) return 0;
            if (fromCurrency === toCurrency) return parseFloat(amount);
            
            // If custom rate provided, use it directly
            if (customRate && fromCurrency !== 'ILS' && toCurrency === 'ILS') {
                return parseFloat(amount) * parseFloat(customRate);
            }
            
            // Convert to USD first, then to target currency
            const fromRate = getExchangeRate(fromCurrency);
            const toRate = getExchangeRate(toCurrency);
            const amountInUSD = parseFloat(amount) / fromRate;
            const amountInTarget = amountInUSD / toRate;
            
            return amountInTarget;
        }
        
        // ==========================================
        // Tabs Management (Multiple Proposals)
        // ==========================================
        function createNewTab(name = null) {
            state.tabCounter++;
            const tabId = 'tab_' + Date.now();
            const tabName = name || `הצעה ${state.tabCounter}`;
            
            const newTab = {
                id: tabId,
                name: tabName,
                flights: [],
                hotels: [],
                services: [],
                commissionValue: state.commissionValue,
                commissionType: state.commissionType,
                manualPriceOverride: null
            };
            
            state.tabs.push(newTab);
            switchToTab(tabId);
            renderTabs();
            return tabId;
        }
        
        function switchToTab(tabId) {
            // Save current tab state first
            if (state.currentTabId) {
                saveCurrentTabState();
            }
            
            // Find and load the new tab
            const tab = state.tabs.find(t => t.id === tabId);
            if (!tab) return;
            
            state.currentTabId = tabId;
            
            // Load tab data into state
            state.flights = tab.flights || [];
            state.hotels = tab.hotels || [];
            state.services = tab.services || [];
            state.commissionValue = tab.commissionValue || 0;
            state.commissionType = tab.commissionType || 'percent';
            state.manualPriceOverride = tab.manualPriceOverride;
            
            // Update UI
            renderFlightCards();
            renderHotelCards();
            renderServiceCards();
            updatePricingTable();
            
            // Update commission UI
            document.getElementById('commissionInput').value = state.commissionValue;
            setCommissionType(state.commissionType);
            
            renderTabs();
        }
        
        function saveCurrentTabState() {
            const tab = state.tabs.find(t => t.id === state.currentTabId);
            if (!tab) return;
            
            tab.flights = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state.flights));
            tab.hotels = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state.hotels));
            tab.services = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state.services));
            tab.commissionValue = state.commissionValue;
            tab.commissionType = state.commissionType;
            tab.manualPriceOverride = state.manualPriceOverride;
        }
        
        function closeTab(tabId, event) {
            if (event) event.stopPropagation();
            
            // Don't close if it's the only tab
            if (state.tabs.length <= 1) {
                showToast('⚠️ לא ניתן לסגור את הטאב האחרון');
                return;
            }
            
            const tabIndex = state.tabs.findIndex(t => t.id === tabId);
            if (tabIndex === -1) return;
            
            // If closing current tab, switch to another first
            if (state.currentTabId === tabId) {
                const newIndex = tabIndex > 0 ? tabIndex - 1 : 1;
                switchToTab(state.tabs[newIndex].id);
            }
            
            state.tabs = state.tabs.filter(t => t.id !== tabId);
            renderTabs();
        }
        
        function renameTab(tabId) {
            const tab = state.tabs.find(t => t.id === tabId);
            if (!tab) return;
            
            const newName = prompt('שם חדש להצעה:', tab.name);
            if (newName && newName.trim()) {
                tab.name = newName.trim();
                renderTabs();
            }
        }
        
        function renderTabs() {
            const container = document.getElementById('tabsContainer');
            if (!container) return;
            
            let html = '';
            state.tabs.forEach(tab => {
                const isActive = tab.id === state.currentTabId;
                html += `
                    <div class="proposal-tab ${isActive ? 'active' : ''}" onclick="switchToTab('${tab.id}')">
                        <span class="tab-name" ondblclick="renameTab('${tab.id}')">${tab.name}</span>
                        <button class="tab-close" onclick="closeTab('${tab.id}', event)" title="סגור">×</button>
                    </div>
                `;
            });
            
            html += `
                <button class="tab-add" onclick="createNewTab()" title="הצעה חדשה">
                    <span>+</span>
                </button>
            `;
            
            container.innerHTML = html;
        }
        
        function initTabs() {
            // Create first tab if none exist
            if (state.tabs.length === 0) {
                createNewTab('הצעה 1');
            } else {
                // Load first tab
                switchToTab(state.tabs[0].id);
            }
        }

```css

    }
}
```

```css
}
```

```css
}

}
```

[Thinking] The user wants to be able to publish each proposal with a unique link that expires after 24-72 hours. This is a significant feature that requires:

1. A backend/server to store the proposals
2. A way to generate unique links
3. Expiration logic

Since this is a static GitHub Pages site, we have several options:

1. **Use a free backend service** like:
   - Firebase (Firestore + Hosting)
   - Supabase
   - JSONbin.io or similar JSON storage APIs
   - Cloudflare Workers + KV

2. **Use a serverless approach** with:
   - Netlify Functions
   - Vercel Edge Functions
   - AWS Lambda

3. **Use a simple pastebin-like service API**

4. **Encode the proposal in the URL itself** (base64 encoded data in URL params) - but this has URL length limits

Since the user has a simple static site on GitHub Pages, I should ask them about their preferences before creating a plan. The simplest solution would probably be to use a free service like Firebase or a simple JSON storage API.

[Tool call] CreatePlan
  name: Publish Proposals with Supabase
  overview: Add ability to publish proposals with unique expiring links using Supabase as the backend storage.
  plan: # Publish Proposals with Expiring Links (Supabase)

## Architecture

```mermaid
flowchart LR
    A[User creates proposal] --> B[Click Publish]
    B --> C[Save to Supabase]
    C --> D[Generate unique link]
    D --> E[Share link with client]
    E --> F[Client opens link]
    F --> G[Load from Supabase]
    G --> H{Expired?}
    H -->|No| I[Show proposal]
    H -->|Yes| J[Show expired message]
```

## Setup Requirements

### 1. Create Supabase Project (Manual Step)
- Go to https://supabase.com and create free account
- Create new project
- Get the `Project URL` and `anon public key` from Settings > API

### 2. Create Database Table
Run this SQL in Supabase SQL Editor:

```sql
CREATE TABLE proposals (
  id UUID DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,
  content TEXT NOT NULL,
  client_name TEXT,
  destination TEXT,
  total_price NUMERIC,
  created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT NOW(),
  expires_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL
);

-- Enable Row Level Security
ALTER TABLE proposals ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Allow anyone to read proposals (they need the UUID)
CREATE POLICY "Public read access" ON proposals FOR SELECT USING (true);

-- Allow anyone to insert (from the app)
CREATE POLICY "Public insert access" ON proposals FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (true);

-- Auto-delete expired proposals (optional - run daily via cron)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION delete_expired_proposals()
RETURNS void AS $$
BEGIN
  DELETE FROM proposals WHERE expires_at < NOW();
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
```

## Code Changes in [index.html](index.html)

### 3. Add Supabase Configuration
Add to state object:

```javascript
supabaseUrl: localStorage.getItem('supabaseUrl') || '',
supabaseKey: localStorage.getItem('supabaseKey') || '',
```

### 4. Add Settings UI for Supabase Keys
Add input fields in the header or settings section for:
- Supabase Project URL
- Supabase Anon Key

### 5. Add Publish Button
Add next to share buttons:

```html
<button class="share-btn publish" onclick="publishProposal()">
    🔗 פרסם הצעה (לינק זמני)
</button>
```

### 6. Add Publish Function

```javascript
async function publishProposal() {
    // Get proposal content
    // Choose expiration (24/48/72 hours)
    // Save to Supabase
    // Generate link: site.com/view.html?id=UUID
    // Copy link to clipboard
}
```

### 7. Create Viewer Page
Create new file `view.html` - a simple page that:
- Reads `id` from URL parameter
- Fetches proposal from Supabase
- Shows proposal or "expired" message
- Has clean, print-friendly design

## Files to Create/Edit
- [index.html](index.html) - Add Supabase config, publish button and function
- `view.html` (new) - Public viewer page for shared proposals

## Expiration Options
User can choose when publishing:
- 24 hours
- 48 hours  
- 72 hours

        // ==========================================
        // Supabase Settings
        // ==========================================
        function toggleSupabaseSettings() {
            const settings = document.getElementById('supabaseSettings');
            settings.style.display = settings.style.display === 'none' ? 'block' : 'none';
            
            // Load current values
            if (settings.style.display === 'block') {
                document.getElementById('supabaseUrlInput').value = state.supabaseUrl;
                document.getElementById('supabaseKeyInput').value = state.supabaseKey;
            }
        }
        
        function saveSupabaseSettings() {
            state.supabaseUrl = document.getElementById('supabaseUrlInput').value.trim();
            state.supabaseKey = document.getElementById('supabaseKeyInput').value.trim();
            
            localStorage.setItem('supabaseUrl', state.supabaseUrl);
            localStorage.setItem('supabaseKey', state.supabaseKey);
            
            document.getElementById('supabaseSettings').style.display = 'none';
            showToast('✅ הגדרות Supabase נשמרו');
        }

  old_string:         function copyProposal() {
            const content = document.getElementById('proposalContent').innerText;
            navigator.clipboard.writeText(content).then(() => {
                showToast('📋 הועתק ללוח');
            }).catch(() => {
                const ta = document.createElement('textarea');
                ta.value = content;
                document.body.appendChild(ta);
                ta.select();
                document.execCommand('copy');
                document.body.removeChild(ta);
                showToast('📋 הועתק ללוח');
            });
        }
        
        /**
         * Generate flight proposal in text format using the saved template
         */
  new_string:         function copyProposal() {
            const content = document.getElementById('proposalContent').innerText;
            navigator.clipboard.writeText(content).then(() => {
                showToast('📋 הועתק ללוח');
            }).catch(() => {
                const ta = document.createElement('textarea');
                ta.value = content;
                document.body.appendChild(ta);
                ta.select();
                document.execCommand('copy');
                document.body.removeChild(ta);
                showToast('📋 הועתק ללוח');
            });
        }
        
        // ==========================================
        // Publish Proposal with Supabase
        // ==========================================
        function showPublishModal() {
            // Ensure proposal is generated first
            const proposalSection = document.getElementById('proposalSection');
            if (!proposalSection || !proposalSection.classList.contains('visible')) {
                showToast('❌ יש ליצור הצעת מחיר קודם');
                return;
            }
            
            // Check Supabase settings
            if (!state.supabaseUrl || !state.supabaseKey) {
                showToast('⚠️ הגדר קודם את Supabase בהגדרות (⚙️)');
                toggleSupabaseSettings();
                return;
            }
            
            document.getElementById('publishModal').classList.add('visible');
        }
        
        function closePublishModal() {
            document.getElementById('publishModal').classList.remove('visible');
        }
        
        async function publishProposal(expiryHours) {
            closePublishModal();
            showToast('⏳ מפרסם הצעה...');
            
            try {
                // Get proposal HTML content
                const proposalContent = document.getElementById('proposalContent').innerHTML;
                const clientName = document.getElementById('clientName').value || 'לקוח';
                const totalPrice = parseFloat(document.getElementById('customerPriceInput').value) || 0;
                
                // Get destination from flights
                let destination = '';
                if (state.flights.length > 0 && state.flights[0].extractedData?.flights) {
                    const flights = state.flights[0].extractedData.flights;
                    if (flights.length > 0) {
                        destination = flights[0].toCity || flights[0].to || '';
                    }
                }
                
                // Calculate expiry time
                const expiresAt = new Date();
                expiresAt.setHours(expiresAt.getHours() + expiryHours);
                
                // Save to Supabase
                const response = await fetch(`${state.supabaseUrl}/rest/v1/proposals`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {
                        'apikey': state.supabaseKey,
                        'Authorization': `Bearer ${state.supabaseKey}`,
                        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                        'Prefer': 'return=representation'
                    },
                    body: JSON.stringify({
                        content: proposalContent,
                        client_name: clientName,
                        destination: destination,
                        total_price: totalPrice,
                        expires_at: expiresAt.toISOString()
                    })
                });
                
                if (!response.ok) {
                    const errorText = await response.text();
                    throw new Error(`שגיאת Supabase: ${response.status} - ${errorText}`);
                }
                
                const data = await response.json();
                const proposalId = data[0]?.id;
                
                if (!proposalId) {
                    throw new Error('לא התקבל ID מ-Supabase');
                }
                
                // Generate the public link
                const baseUrl = window.location.origin + window.location.pathname.replace('index.html', '');
                const publicLink = `${baseUrl}view.html?id=${proposalId}`;
                
                // Copy to clipboard
                await navigator.clipboard.writeText(publicLink);
                
                showToast(`✅ הצעה פורסמה! הלינק הועתק (${expiryHours} שעות)`);
                
                // Show the link in an alert for easy access
                alert(`הלינק להצעה:\n\n${publicLink}\n\nהלינק הועתק ללוח!\nתוקף: ${expiryHours} שעות`);
                
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Publish error:', error);
                showToast('❌ שגיאה בפרסום: ' + error.message);
            }
        }
        
        /**
         * Generate flight proposal in text format using the saved template
         */

    <script>
        // Get Supabase config from URL or localStorage
        const SUPABASE_URL = localStorage.getItem('supabaseUrl') || '';
        const SUPABASE_KEY = localStorage.getItem('supabaseKey') || '';
        
        async function loadProposal() {
            const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
            const proposalId = urlParams.get('id');
            
            if (!proposalId) {
                showError('לא צוין מזהה הצעה');
                return;
            }
            
            if (!SUPABASE_URL || !SUPABASE_KEY) {
                showError('הגדרות Supabase חסרות');
                return;
            }
            
            try {
                const response = await fetch(
                    `${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/proposals?id=eq.${proposalId}&select=*`,
                    {
                        headers: {
                            'apikey': SUPABASE_KEY,
                            'Authorization': `Bearer ${SUPABASE_KEY}`
                        }
                    }
                );
                
                if (!response.ok) {
                    throw new Error('שגיאה בטעינת ההצעה');
                }
                
                const data = await response.json();
                
                if (!data || data.length === 0) {
                    showError('ההצעה לא נמצאה');
                    return;
                }
                
                const proposal = data[0];
                
                // Check if expired
                const expiresAt = new Date(proposal.expires_at);
                if (expiresAt < new Date()) {
                    showExpired();
                    return;
                }
                
                // Show proposal
                showProposal(proposal);
                
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error loading proposal:', error);
                showError(error.message);
            }
        }
        
        function showError(message) {
            document.getElementById('loadingState').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('errorMessage').textContent = message;
            document.getElementById('errorState').style.display = 'block';
        }
        
        function showExpired() {
            document.getElementById('loadingState').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('expiredState').style.display = 'block';
        }
        
        function showProposal(proposal) {
            document.getElementById('loadingState').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('proposalContent').innerHTML = proposal.content;
            document.getElementById('proposalState').style.display = 'block';
            
            // Update page title
            if (proposal.client_name) {
                document.title = `הצעה עבור ${proposal.client_name} | Lord Tickets`;
            }
        }
        
        // Load proposal when page loads
        loadProposal();
    </script>
</body>
</html>

## מה נוסף:

## איך להגדיר Supabase (חד פעמי):

### שלב 1: יצירת פרויקט
1. לך ל-https://supabase.com ופתח חשבון חינמי
2. צור פרויקט חדש

### שלב 2: יצירת טבלה
הריץ את ה-SQL הזה ב-SQL Editor של Supabase:

```sql
CREATE TABLE proposals (
  id UUID DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,
  content TEXT NOT NULL,
  client_name TEXT,
  destination TEXT,
  total_price NUMERIC,
  created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT NOW(),
  expires_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL
);

ALTER TABLE proposals ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "Public read access" ON proposals FOR SELECT USING (true);
CREATE POLICY "Public insert access" ON proposals FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (true);
```

### שלב 3: הזנת מפתחות באפליקציה
1. לך ל-Settings > API בפרויקט Supabase
2. העתק את `Project URL` ו-`anon public key`
3. באפליקציה, לחץ ⚙️ והזן את הפרטים

+        </div>

+        <div id="errorState" class="error-container" style="display: none;">
+            <div class="error-icon">❌</div>
+            <div class="error-title">שגיאה</div>
+            <div class="error-message" id="errorMessage">לא ניתן לטעון את ההצעה</div>
+        </div>
+        
+        <div id="expiredState" class="expired-container" style="display: none;">
+            <div class="expired-icon">⏰</div>
+            <div class="expired-title">ההצעה פגה</div>
+            <div class="expired-message">הלינק להצעה זו כבר לא בתוקף.<br>אנא פנה לסוכן לקבלת הצעה מעודכנת.</div>
+        </div>
+        
+        <div id="proposalState" style="display: none;">
+            <div class="proposal-container">
+                <div class="proposal-header">
+                    <div class="proposal-logo">✈️ Lord Tickets</div>
+                    <div class="proposal-title">הצעת מחיר</div>
+                </div>
+                <div class="proposal-content" id="proposalContent">
+                    <!-- Proposal content will be loaded here -->
+                </div>
+            </div>
+            <button class="print-btn" onclick="window.print()">🖨️ הדפס / שמור PDF</button>
+        </div>
+    </main>
+    
+    <footer class="footer">
+        <p>הצעה מ-<a href="index.html">Lord Tickets</a></p>
+    </footer>
+    
+    <script>
+        // Get Supabase config from URL or localStorage
+        const SUPABASE_URL = localStorage.getItem('supabaseUrl') || '';
+        const SUPABASE_KEY = localStorage.getItem('supabaseKey') || '';
+        
+        async function loadProposal() {
+            const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
+            const proposalId = urlParams.get('id');
+            
+            if (!proposalId) {
+                showError('לא צוין מזהה הצעה');
+                return;
+            }
+            
+            if (!SUPABASE_URL || !SUPABASE_KEY) {
+                showError('הגדרות Supabase חסרות');
+                return;
+            }
+            
+            try {
+                const response = await fetch(
+                    `${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/proposals?id=eq.${proposalId}&select=*`,
+                    {
+                        headers: {
+                            'apikey': SUPABASE_KEY,
+                            'Authorization': `Bearer ${SUPABASE_KEY}`
+                        }
+                    }
+                );
+                
+                if (!response.ok) {
+                    throw new Error('שגיאה בטעינת ההצעה');
+                }
+                
+                const data = await response.json();
+                
+                if (!data || data.length === 0) {
+                    showError('ההצעה לא נמצאה');
+                    return;
+                }
+                
+                const proposal = data[0];
+                
+                // Check if expired
+                const expiresAt = new Date(proposal.expires_at);
+                if (expiresAt < new Date()) {
+                    showExpired();
+                    return;
+                }
+                
+                // Show proposal
+                showProposal(proposal);
+                
+            } catch (error) {
+                console.error('Error loading proposal:', error);
+                showError(error.message);
+            }
+        }
+        
+        function showError(message) {
+            document.getElementById('loadingState').style.display = 'none';
+            document.getElementById('errorMessage').textContent = message;
+            document.getElementById('errorState').style.display = 'block';
+        }
+        
+        function showExpired() {
+            document.getElementById('loadingState').style.display = 'none';
+            document.getElementById('expiredState').style.display = 'block';
+        }
+        
+        function showProposal(proposal) {
+            document.getElementById('loadingState').style.display = 'none';
+            document.getElementById('proposalContent').innerHTML = proposal.content;
+            document.getElementById('proposalState').style.display = 'block';
+            
+            // Update page title
+            if (proposal.client_name) {
+                document.title = `הצעה עבור ${proposal.client_name} | Lord Tickets`;
+            }
+        }
+        
+        // Load proposal when page loads
+        loadProposal();
+    </script>
+</body>
+</html>
